== supplementary groups
/proc/self/setgroups: allow

root setgroups(2, {2000, 2001}) = 0 -> groups '2000 2001'
root getgroups(0, NULL) = 2, getgroups(1, list) = -22, getgroups(2, list) = 2 (2000 2001), getgroups(8, list) = 2, getgroups(-1, list) = -22
